# Mage's Commune copies of the merchant company buildings (vanilla 00_merchant_company_buildings.txt, keys prefixed mages_commune_)

@merchant_company_main_construction_time_02 = 365
@merchant_company_main_construction_time_03 = 545
@merchant_company_main_construction_time_04 = 730
@merchant_company_main_construction_time_05 = 915
@merchant_company_building_construction_time = 365
@merchant_company_external_construction_time_02 = 120
@merchant_company_external_construction_time_03 = 240
@merchant_company_external_construction_time_04 = 360
@merchant_company_external_construction_time_05 = 480
@merchant_company_external_construction_time_06 = 600
@merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_01 = 90
@merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_02 = 120
@merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_03 = 150
@merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_04 = 180
@merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_05 = 210
@company_capacity_add = 3
@company_capacity_add_combo = 1
@company_capacity_mult = 0.02
@company_sell_price_all = 0.005
@company_sell_price_all_half = 0.0025
@company_buy_price_all = -0.005
@company_sell_price_category = 0.0025
@company_buy_price_category = -0.0025
@company_buy_price_category_half = -0.00125
@company_buy_price_all_half = -0.0025
@company_sell_price_renown = 0.005
@company_buy_price_renown = -0.005
@company_sell_price_renown_mid = 0.0075
@company_buy_price_renown_mid = -0.0075
@company_sell_price_renown_cheap = 0.01
@company_buy_price_renown_cheap = -0.01
@company_sell_price_category_mid = 0.00375
@company_buy_price_category_mid = -0.00375
@company_sell_price_category_cheap = 0.005
@company_buy_price_category_cheap = -0.005
@company_prestige_standalone_minor = 0.01
@company_prestige_standalone = 0.02

mages_commune_merchant_company_main_01 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = main
	internal_slots = 3
	construction_time = 1

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_external_slots_capacity_add = 4
		domicile_monthly_gold_add = 0.5
		negate_health_penalty_add = 0.6
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_main_upgrade_value
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_western_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_western_villa.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_villa_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_villa_western_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_asian_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_asian_villa.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_villa_asian.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_villa_asian_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_mena_villa.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_villa_mena.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_villa_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_main_02 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = main
	internal_slots = 4
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_main_01
	construction_time = @merchant_company_main_construction_time_02

	cost = {
		prestige = {
			if = {
				limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_merchant }
				value = merchant_company_main_prestige_cost_tier_2_value
			}
			else = { value = 0 }
		}
		gold = merchant_company_main_cost_tier_2_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_external_slots_capacity_add = 1
		domicile_monthly_gold_add = 0.1
		max_trade_routes = 1
		trade_route_length_add = 2
		negate_health_penalty_add = 0.1
		monthly_dynasty_prestige = 0.15
		prestige_standalone_gain_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_main_upgrade_value
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_western_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_western_manor.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_manor_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_manor_western_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_asian_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_asian_manor.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_manor_asian.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_manor_asian_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_mena_manor.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_manor_mena.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_manor_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_main_03 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = main
	internal_slots = 5
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_main_02

	construction_time = @merchant_company_main_construction_time_03

	can_construct = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_merchant }
			OR = {
				has_realm_law = company_size_medium
				has_realm_law = company_size_large
				has_realm_law = company_size_huge
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		prestige = {
			if = {
				limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_merchant }
				value = merchant_company_main_prestige_cost_tier_3_value
			}
			else = { value = 0 }
		}
		gold = merchant_company_main_cost_tier_3_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_external_slots_capacity_add = 2
		domicile_monthly_gold_add = 0.1
		trade_route_length_add = 2
		negate_health_penalty_add = 0.1
		monthly_dynasty_prestige = 0.15
		prestige_standalone_gain_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_main_upgrade_value
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_western_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_western_mansion.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_mansion_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_mansion_western_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_asian_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_asian_mansion.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_mansion_asian.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_mansion_asian_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_mena_mansion.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_mansion_mena.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_mansion_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_main_04 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = main
	internal_slots = 6
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_main_03

	construction_time = @merchant_company_main_construction_time_04

	can_construct = {
		# The Magocracy has no republic tier laws (republic_tier_*): its realm tier is the head's highest title
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_magocracy }
			top_liege = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom }
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_merchant }
			OR = {
				has_realm_law = company_size_large
				has_realm_law = company_size_huge
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		prestige = {
			if = {
				limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_merchant }
				value = merchant_company_main_prestige_cost_tier_4_value
			}
			else = { value = 0 }
		}
		gold = merchant_company_main_cost_tier_4_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_external_slots_capacity_add = 3
		domicile_monthly_gold_add = 0.15
		max_trade_routes = 1
		trade_route_length_add = 3
		negate_health_penalty_add = 0.1
		monthly_dynasty_prestige = 0.15
		prestige_standalone_gain_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_main_upgrade_value
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_western_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_western_estate.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_estate_western_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_asian_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_asian_estate.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_estate_asian.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_estate_asian_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_mena_estate.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_estate_mena.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_estate_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_main_05 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = main
	internal_slots = 7
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_main_04

	construction_time = @merchant_company_main_construction_time_05

	can_construct = {
		# The Magocracy has no republic tier laws (republic_tier_*): its realm tier is the head's highest title
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_magocracy }
			top_liege = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_empire }
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_merchant }
			has_realm_law = company_size_huge
		}
	}

	cost = {
		prestige = {
			if = {
				limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_merchant }
				value = merchant_company_main_prestige_cost_tier_5_value
			}
			else = { value = 0 }
		}
		gold = merchant_company_main_cost_tier_5_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_external_slots_capacity_add = 4
		domicile_monthly_gold_add = 0.15
		trade_route_length_add = 3
		negate_health_penalty_add = 0.1
		monthly_dynasty_prestige = 0.15
		prestige_standalone_gain_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_main_upgrade_value
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_western_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_western_grand_estate.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		trigger = {
			company_domicile_asian_asset_trigger = yes
		}
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_asian_grand_estate.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_asian.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_asian_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_merchant_mena_grand_estate.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_mena.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_estate_main_building"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_ballroom_01 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	can_construct_potential = {
		government_has_flag = government_is_republic
	}
	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_main_01
	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_01

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_1_value
	}

	parameters = {
		company_patrician_rating_bonus_1 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_normal_new_internal_value
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_trailing_musicians.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_ballroom_02 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	can_construct_potential = {
		government_has_flag = government_is_republic
	}
	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_ballroom_01
	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_02

	can_construct = {
		domicile ?= { has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_main_02 }
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_2_value
	}

	parameters = {
		company_patrician_rating_bonus_2 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_normal_internal_value

		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_common_chest_03 = {

		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_3_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		character_capital_popular_opinion_add = 4
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_normal_internal_value

	}

		domicile_stewardship_education_bonus_03 = yes

	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_luxury_stalls_01
	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_02

	can_construct = {
		domicile ?= { has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_trade_02 }
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_2_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		trade_category_luxury_consumption_xp_gain_mult = 0.05
		trade_category_incense_xp_gain_mult = 0.05
		trade_category_books_xp_gain_mult = 0.05
		trade_category_medicaments_xp_gain_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_normal_internal_value
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_trade_expertise_luxuries.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"
	}
}

mages_commune_merchant_company_sugar_traders_03 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_sugar_traders_02
	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_03

	can_construct = {
		domicile ?= { has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_trade_03 }
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_3_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		trade_good_sugar_sell_price_mult = @company_sell_price_category
		trade_good_sugar_buy_price_mult = @company_buy_price_category
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_avoided_internal_value
	}

	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_caravan_yard_01
	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_02

	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile ?= {
			domicile_location.county ?= { is_coastal_county = no }
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile ?= { has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_warehouse_02 }
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_2_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		trade_capacity_add = @company_capacity_add_combo
		trade_category_beasts_of_burden_buy_price_mult = @company_buy_price_category_mid
		trade_route_length_add = 1
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_preferred_internal_value
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_camel_run.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"
	}
}

	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_05

	can_construct = {
		mages_commune_merchant_company_external_unlocked_by_main_tier_05_trigger = yes
		domicile ?= {
			OR = {
				has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_civic_05_stress
				has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_civic_05_knights
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_5_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		character_travel_safety = 10
		character_travel_speed = 5
		travel_companion_opinion = 3
	}

	parameters = {
		domicile_unlock_trusted_lodgings_travel_option = yes
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_avoided_internal_value
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_guest.dds"

	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_guardhouse_03

	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_04

	can_construct = {
		mages_commune_merchant_company_external_unlocked_by_main_tier_04_trigger = yes
		domicile ?= { has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_civic_04 }
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_4_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		knight_limit = 1
		knight_effectiveness_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_avoided_internal_value
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_guardhouse.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"
	}
}

	slot_type = internal

	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_05

	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_05

	can_construct = {
		mages_commune_merchant_company_external_unlocked_by_main_tier_05_trigger = yes
		domicile ?= {
			OR = {
				has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_civic_05_stress
				has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_civic_05_knights
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_5_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		monthly_intrigue_lifestyle_xp_gain_add = 2
		owned_hostile_scheme_success_chance_add = 3
	}

	parameters = {
		domicile_intrigue_education_bonus_03 = yes
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_avoided_internal_value
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_juicy_rumors.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"

mages_commune_merchant_company_salt_office_05 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_salt_office_04
	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_05

	can_construct = {
		gdp_renown_building_expertise_trigger = { CATEGORY = trade_category_salt }
		domicile ?= {
			OR = {
				has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_production_05_prestige
				has_domicile_building_or_higher = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_production_05_income
			}
		}
	}

	cost = {
		gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_5_value
	}

	character_modifier = {
		trade_good_salt_sell_price_mult = @company_sell_price_renown
		trade_good_salt_buy_price_mult = @company_buy_price_renown
		trade_route_length_add = 1
	}

	ai_value = {
		value = merchant_company_ai_avoided_internal_value
	}

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/building_types/resource_buildings/salt_buildings.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"

mages_commune_merchant_company_artisan_capstone_01 = {
	allowed_domicile_types = { mages_commune }
	slot_type = internal
	previous_building = mages_commune_merchant_company_building_production_01
	construction_time = @merchant_company_upgrade_construction_time_01

	cost = { gold = merchant_company_upgrade_cost_tier_4_value }

	can_construct = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = merchant_company_artisan_capstone_01_tt
			exists = primary_title.var:merchant_company_artisan_capstone_unlocked
		}
	}

	character_modifier = {
		artifact_decay_reduction_mult = -0.35
		trade_good_jewelry_sell_price_mult = 0.05
		trade_good_glass_sell_price_mult = 0.05
		trade_good_ceramics_sell_price_mult = 0.05
		trade_good_lacquerware_sell_price_mult = 0.05
		trade_good_textiles_sell_price_mult = 0.05
		trade_good_books_paper_sell_price_mult = 0.05
		trade_expertise_growth_mult = 0.15
		domicile_monthly_prestige_mult = 0.1
	}
	
	parameters = {
		company_better_inspiration_quality = yes
		company_better_artifact_schemes = yes
	}
	
	ai_value = { value = merchant_company_ai_capstone_value }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/artisan_capstone.dds" # TODO_CD_GDP
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/merchant_company_grand_estate_western_mask.png"
	}
